<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Huraira (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne rukhsat di Ariyon ke bechne ki atkal se bashart-e-ke paanch wasq se kam hon ya paanch wasq ke andar hon. Imam Malik ne kaha Ariyah ka andaza darakhton par kar liya jayega aur An-Hazrat (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne isko jaiz rakha. Ye tawliya ya iqala ya shirkat ke misl hai. Agar ye aur bayon ke misl hota to khane ki cheezon ka tawliya ya iqala ya shirkat qabl-e-qabza ke nadorust hai, ye bhi durust na hota.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1304]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1304" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1291

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1305)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ أَبِي الرِّجَالِ، مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ عَنْ أُمِّهِ، عَمْرَةَ بِنْتِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ أَنَّهُ سَمِعَهَا تَقُولُ، ابْتَاعَ رَجُلٌ ثَمَرَ حَائِطٍ فِي زَمَانِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَعَالَجَهُ وَقَامَ فِيهِ حَتَّى تَبَيَّنَ لَهُ النُّقْصَانُ فَسَأَلَ رَبَّ الْحَائِطِ أَنْ يَضَعَ لَهُ أَوْ أَنْ يُقِيلَهُ فَحَلَفَ أَنْ لاَ يَفْعَلَ فَذَهَبَتْ أُمُّ الْمُشْتَرِي إِلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَذَكَرَتْ ذَلِكَ لَهُ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ تَأَلَّى أَنْ لاَ يَفْعَلَ خَيْرًا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَسَمِعَ بِذَلِكَ رَبُّ الْحَائِطِ فَأَتَى رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَقَالَ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ هُوَ لَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1305]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1305" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Abu&#x27;r-Rijal Muhammad ibn Abd ar-Rahman heard his mother, Amra bint Abd ar-Rahman say, &quot;A man bought the fruit of an enclosed orchard in the time of the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, and he tended it while staying on the land. It became clear to him that there was going to be some loss. He asked the owner of the orchard to reduce the price for him or to revoke the sale, but the owner made an oath not to do so. The mother of the buyer went to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, and told him about it. The Messengerof Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;By this oath, he has sworn not to do good.&#x27; The owner of the orchard heard about it and went to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, and said, &#x27;Messenger of Allah, the choice is his</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1305]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1305" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अम्र बिन्त अब्दुर रहमान (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के ज़माने में एक शख्स ने बाग़ के फल ख़रीदे और उसकी दुरुस्ती में मसरूफ हुआ मगर ऐसी आफत आयी जिससे नुकसान मालूम हुआ तो बाघ के मालिक से कहा या तो फलों की कीमत कुछ कम कर दो या इस बे को फ़स्ख़ कर डालो. उसने क़सम कहा ली मैं हरगिज़ न करूँगा. तब खरीदार की माँ ने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से आ कर ये सब किस्सा बयां किया. आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया क्या क़सम खा ली उसने के मैं ये बेहतरी का काम न करूँगा? जब मालिक-इ-बाघ को ये खबर पहुंची वो रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के पास आया और अर्ज़ किया या रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) जैसा खरीदार कहे वो मुझको मंज़ूर है. उमर बिन अब्दुल अज़ीज़ ने हुक्म किया मुश्तरी को नुक़्सान दिलाने का जब खेत या मेवे को आफत पहुंचे. इमाम मलिक ने कहा हमारे नज़दीक यही हुक्म है लेकिन ये ज़रूर है के उस आफत से तिहाई माल या ज़्यादा नुक़सान हुआ हो</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1305]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1305" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عمرہ بنت عبدالرحمن رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے زمانے میں ایک شخص نے باغ کے پھل خریدے اور اس کی درستی میں مصروف ہوا مگر ایسی آفت آئی جس سے نقصان معلوم ہوا تو باغ کے مالک سے کہا یا تو پھلوں کی قیمت کچھ کم کر دو یا اس بیع کو فسخ کر ڈالو اس نے قسم کھالی میں ہرگز نہ کروں گا تب خریداری کر ڈالو اس نے قسم کھالی میں ہرگز نہ کروں گا تب خریدار کی ماں نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے آن کر یہ سب قصہ بیان کیا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا کیا قسم کھالی اس نے کہ میں یہ بہتری کا کام نہ کروں گا جب مالک باغ کو یہ خبر پہنچی وہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے پاس آیا اور عرض کیا یا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم جیسا خریدار کہے وہ مجھ کو منظور ہے ۔ عمر بن عبدالعزیز نے حکم کیا مشتری (خریدنے والا) کو نقصان دلانے کا جب کھیت یا میوے کو آفت پہنچے ۔ کہا مالک نے ہمارے نزدیک یہی حکم ہے لیکن یہ ضرور ہے کہ اس آفت سے تہائی مال یا زیادہ نقصان ہوا ہو اگر اس سے کم نقصان ہوگا اس کا شمار نہیں ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1305]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1305" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Amra bint Abdur Rahman (Radi Allahu Anha) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke zamane mein ek shakhs ne bagh ke phal kharide aur uski durusti mein masroof hua magar aisi aafat aayi jisse nuqsan maloom hua to bagh ke malik se kaha ya to phalon ki qeemat kuch kam kar do ya is bay ko faskh kar dalo. Usne qasam kha li main hargiz na karunga. Tab kharidar ki maa ne Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se aa kar ye sab qissa bayan kiya. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya kya qasam kha li usne ke main ye behtari ka kaam na karunga? Jab malik-e-bagh ko ye khabar pahunchi wo Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke paas aaya aur arz kiya ya Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) jaisa kharidar kahe wo mujhko manzoor hai. Umar bin Abdul Aziz ne hukm kiya mushtari ko nuqsan dilane ka jab khet ya meway ko aafat pahunche. Imam Malik ne kaha hamare nazdeek yahi hukm hai lekin ye zaroor hai ke us aafat se tihai maal ya zyada nuqsan hua ho, agar isse kam nuqsan hoga to uska shumar nahi.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1305]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1305" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1292

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1306)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1306]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1306" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1306</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1306 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ عَبْدِ الْعَزِيزِ، قَضَى بِوَضْعِ الْجَائِحَةِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَعَلَى ذَلِكَ الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَالْجَائِحَةُ الَّتِي تُوضَعُ عَنِ الْمُشْتَرِي الثُّلُثُ فَصَاعِدًا وَلاَ يَكُونُ مَا دُونَ ذَلِكَ جَائِحَةً ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1306" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: historical report. Attribution: Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz decided in a case to make a reduction for crop damage.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1306]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1306" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1306</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1306 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;That is what we do in the situation.&quot; Malik added, &quot;Crop damage is whatever causes loss of a third or more for the purchaser. Anything less is not counted as crop damage</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1306" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

</section>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1306 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">याह्या ने मुझे मालिक से बयां किया कि उन्होंने सुना कि उमर इब्न अब्दुल अज़ीज़ ने फसल के नुकसान के मामले में कमी करने का फैसला किया. मलिक ने कहा: &#x27;हम इस सूरतेहाल में ऐसा ही करते हैं.&#x27; मलिक ने मज़ीद कहा: &#x27;फ़सल का नुक़सान वह है जिस से खरीदार को एक तिहाई या उस से ज्यादा का नुक्सान हो</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1306" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مجھے مالک سے بیان کیا کہ انہوں نے سنا کہ عمر بن عبدالعزیز نے فصل کے نقصان کے معاملے میں کمی کرنے کا فیصلہ کیا۔ مالک نے کہا: &#x27;ہم اس صورتحال میں ایسا ہی کرتے ہیں۔&#x27; مالک نے مزید کہا: &#x27;فصل کا نقصان وہ ہے جس سے خریدار کو ایک تہائی یا اس سے زیادہ کا نقصان ہو، اس سے کم کو فصل کا نقصان شمار نہیں کیا جاتا۔&#x27;</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1306" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne mujhe Malik se bayan kiya ke unhone suna ke Umar ibn Abdul Aziz ne fasal ke nuqsan ke mamle mein kami karne ka faisla kiya. Malik ne kaha: &#x27;Hum is sooratehal mein aisa hi karte hain.&#x27; Malik ne mazeed kaha: &#x27;Fasal ka nuqsan woh hai jis se kharidar ko ek tihai ya us se zyada ka nuqsan ho, us se kam ko fasal ka nuqsan shumar nahi kiya jata.&#x27;</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1306" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: historical report. Attribution: Muhammad ibn Amr ibn Hazm (unresolved). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abdullah ibn Abi Bakr that his grandfather, Muhammad ibn Amr ibn Hazm sold the fruit of an orchard of his called al-Afraq, for 4,000 dirhams, and he kept aside 800 dirhams&#x27; worth of dry dates</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1308]</p>

## Narration 1295

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1309)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1309]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1309" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1309</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1309 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ أَبِي الرِّجَالِ، مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ حَارِثَةَ أَنَّ أُمَّهُ، عَمْرَةَ بِنْتَ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ كَانَتْ تَبِيعُ ثِمَارَهَا وَتَسْتَثْنِي مِنْهَا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ الأَمْرُ الْمُجْتَمَعُ عَلَيْهِ عِنْدَنَا أَنَّ الرَّجُلَ إِذَا بَاعَ ثَمَرَ حَائِطِهِ أَنَّ لَهُ أَنْ يَسْتَثْنِيَ مِنْ ثَمَرِ حَائِطِهِ مَا بَيْنَهُ وَبَيْنَ ثُلُثِ الثَّمَرِ لاَ يُجَاوِزُ ذَلِكَ وَمَا كَانَ دُونَ الثُّلُثِ فَلاَ بَأْسَ بِذَلِكَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فَأَمَّا الرَّجُلُ يَبِيعُ ثَمَرَ حَائِطِهِ وَيَسْتَثْنِي مِنْ ثَمَرِ حَائِطِهِ ثَمَرَ نَخْلَةٍ أَوْ نَخَلاَتٍ يَخْتَارُهَا وَيُسَمِّي عَدَدَهَا فَلاَ أَرَى بِذَلِكَ بَأْسًا لأَنَّ رَبَّ الْحَائِطِ إِنَّمَا اسْتَثْنَى شَيْئًا مِنْ ثَمَرِ حَائِطِ نَفْسِهِ وَإِنَّمَا ذَلِكَ شَىْءٌ احْتَبَسَهُ مِنْ حَائِطِهِ وَأَمْسَكَهُ لَمْ يَبِعْهُ وَبَاعَ مِنْ حَائِطِهِ مَا سِوَى ذَلِكَ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1309" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: historical report. Attribution: Amra bint Abd ar-Rahman in her sons report of her fruit sales. Select the actual reported practice of Amra keeping some fruit aside when selling it. The following general rules and preference of Malik remain compiler commentary.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abu&#x27;r-Rijal, Muhammad ibn Abdar-Rahman ibn Haritha that his mother, Amra bint Abd ar-Rahman used to sell her fruit and keep some of it aside. </p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1309]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1309" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1309</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1309 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Malik said, &quot;The generally agreed upon way of doing things among us is that when a man sells the fruit of his orchard, he can keep aside up to a third of the fruit, but that is not to be exceeded. There is no harm in what is less than a third.&quot; Malik added that he thought there was no harm for a man to sell the fruit of his orchard and keep aside only the fruit of a certain palm-tree or palm-trees which he had chosen and whose number he had specified, because the owner was only keeping aside certain fruit of his own orchard and everything else he sold</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1309" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1309</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1309 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अमरा बिंत अब्दुर रहमान अपने फलों को बेचतिं और उस में से कुछ निकाल लेतीं। इमाम मालिक ने कहा हमारे नज़दीक ये हुक्म इत्तेफ़ाक़ी है के जो आदमी अपने बाग़ का मेवा बेचे उसको इख़्तियार है के तिहाई माल तक मुस्तसना करे, इस से ज़्यादा दुरुस्त नहीं। और जो सारे बाग़ में से एक दरख़्त या दरख़्त के फल मुस्तसना करले और उसको मुअय्यन कर दे तो भी कुछ क़बाहत नहीं है, क्यूंके गोया मालिक ने सिवाए उन दरख़्तों के बाक़ी को बेचा और उनको न बेचा, इस अमर का मालिक को इख़्तियार है।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1309" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">عمرہ بنت عبدالرحمن اپنے پھلوں کو بیچتیں اور اس میں سے کچھ نکال لیتیں ۔ کہا مالک نے ہمارے نزدیک یہ حکم اتفاقی ہے کہ جو آدمی اپنے باغ کا میوہ بچے اس کو اختیار ہے کہ تہائی مال تک مستثنیٰ کرے اس سے زیادہ درست نہیں اور جو سارے باغ میں سے ایک درخت یا درخت کے پھل مستثنی کرلے اور اس کو معین کر دے تو بھی کچھ قباحت نہیں ہے کیونکہ گویا مالک نے سوائے ان درختوں کے باقی کو بیچا اور ان کو نہ بیچا اس امر کا مالک کو اختیار ہے۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1309" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Amra bint Abdur Rahman apne phalon ko bechtin aur usmein se kuch nikal letin. Imam Malik ne kaha hamare nazdeek ye hukm ittefaqi hai ke jo aadmi apne bagh ka mewa beche usko ikhtiyar hai ke tihai maal tak mustasna kare, isse zyada durust nahi. Aur jo saare bagh mein se ek darakht ya darakht ke phal mustasna karle aur usko muayyan kar de to bhi kuch qabahut nahi hai, kyunke goya malik ne siwaye un darakhton ke baqi ko becha aur unko na becha, is amar ka malik ko ikhtiyar hai.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1309" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1296

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1310)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ، عَنْ عَطَاءِ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ التَّمْرُ بِالتَّمْرِ مِثْلاً بِمِثْلٍ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَقِيلَ لَهُ إِنَّ عَامِلَكَ عَلَى خَيْبَرَ يَأْخُذُ الصَّاعَ بِالصَّاعَيْنِ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ ادْعُوهُ لِي ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَدُعِيَ لَهُ فَقَالَ لَهُ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ أَتَأْخُذُ الصَّاعَ بِالصَّاعَيْنِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ لاَ يَبِيعُونَنِي الْجَنِيبَ بِالْجَمْعِ صَاعًا بِصَاعٍ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ لَهُ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ بِعِ الْجَمْعَ بِالدَّرَاهِمِ ثُمَّ ابْتَعْ بِالدَّرَاهِمِ جَنِيبًا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1310]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1310" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Zayd ibn Aslam that Ata ibn Yasar said, &quot;The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;Dried dates for dried dates is like for like.&#x27; It was said to him, &#x27;Your agent in Khaybar takes one sa for two.&#x27; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;all him to me.&#x27; So he was called for. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, asked, &#x27;Do you take one sa for two?&#x27; He replied, &#x27;Messengerof Allah! Why should they sell me good dates for assorted low quality dates, sa for sa!&#x27; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;Sell the assorted ones for dirhams, and then buy the good ones with those dirhams</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1310]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1310" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अता बिन यसर से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया के खजूर को खजूर के बदले में बराबर बराबर बेचो. एक शख्स बोलै या रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) आपको आमिल-इ-खैबारी एक सा&#x27;आ खजूर लेकर दो सा&#x27;आ देता है सा&#x27;आ बेहतर खजूर और एक सा&#x27;ए बुरी खजूर के बदले में नहीं आती? आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया पहले बुरी खजूर को रुपयों के बदले में बेच कर फिर उम्दा खजूर को खरीद कर ले.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1310]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1310" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عطاء بن یسار سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا کہ کھجور کو کھجور کے بدلے میں برابر برابر بیچو ایک شخص بولا یا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم آپ کو عامل خبری پر ایک صاع کھجور لے کر دو صاع دیتا ہے کھجور دے کر ایک صاع لیتا ہے وہ بولا یا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم ایک صاع بہتر کھجور اور ایک صاع بری کھجور کے بدلے میں نہیں آتی آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا پہلے بری کھجور کو روپوں کے بدلے میں بیچ کر پھر عمدہ کھجور کو خرید کر لے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1310]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1310" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Ataa bin Yasar se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya ke khajoor ko khajoor ke badle mein barabar barabar becho. Ek shakhs bola ya Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) aapko Aamil-e-Khaibari ek sa&#x27;a khajoor lekar do sa&#x27;a deta hai, khajoor dekar ek sa&#x27;a leta hai. Wo bola ya Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ek sa&#x27;a behtar khajoor aur ek sa&#x27;a buri khajoor ke badle mein nahi aati? Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya pehle buri khajoor ko rupayon ke badle mein bech kar phir umda khajoor ko kharid kar le.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1310]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1310" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1297

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1311)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الْحَمِيدِ بْنِ سُهَيْلِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ عَوْفٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيَّبِ، عَنْ أَبِي سَعِيدٍ الْخُدْرِيِّ، وَعَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم اسْتَعْمَلَ رَجُلاً عَلَى خَيْبَرَ فَجَاءَهُ بِتَمْرٍ جَنِيبٍ فَقَالَ لَهُ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ أَكُلُّ تَمْرِ خَيْبَرَ هَكَذَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ لاَ وَاللَّهِ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ إِنَّا لَنَأْخُذُ الصَّاعَ مِنْ هَذَا بِالصَّاعَيْنِ وَالصَّاعَيْنِ بِالثَّلاَثَةِ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ لاَ تَفْعَلْ بِعِ الْجَمْعَ بِالدَّرَاهِمِ ثُمَّ ابْتَعْ بِالدَّرَاهِمِ جَنِيبًا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1311]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1311" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abd al-Hamid ibn Suhayl ibn Abd ar-Rahman ibn Awf from Said ibn al-Musayyab from Abu Said al- Khudri and from Abu Hurayra that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, appointed a man as an agent in Khaybar, and he brought him some excellent dates. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said to him, &quot;Are all the dates of Khaybar like this?&quot; He said,&quot;No. By Allah, Messenger of Allah! We take a sa of this kind for two sa or two sa for three.&quot; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Do not do that. Sell the assorted ones for dirhams and then buy the good ones with the dirhams</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1311]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1311" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू सईद (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) और अबू हुरैरह (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने एक शख़्स को ख़ैबर पर आमिल मुक़र्रर किया, वो उम्दा खजूर लेकर आया। आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने पूछा: क्या सब खजूरें ख़ैबर की ऐसी ही होती हैं? उस ने कहा: नहीं या रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम), हम इस खजूर में से एक सा दो सा के बदले में या दो सा तीन सा के बदले में खरीद लिया करते हैं। आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: ऐसा न करो, पहले बुरी खजूर को रुपयों के बदले में बेच कर फिर उम्दा खजूर रुपये देकर खरीद लो।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1311]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1311" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابو سعید اور ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے ایک شخص کو عامل مقرر کیا خیبر پر وہ عمدہ کھجور لے کر آیا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے پوچھا سب کھجوریں خبیر کی ایسی ہی ہوتی ہیں وہ بولا نہیں یا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم ہم اس کھجور میں سے ایک صاع دو صاع کے بدلے میں یا دو صاع تین صاع کے بدلے میں خرید کیا کرتے ہیں آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا ایسا نہ کرے پہلے بری کھجور کو روپوں کے بدلے میں بیچ کر پھر عمدہ کھجور روپے دے کر خرید لے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1311]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1311" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Saeed (Radi Allahu Anhu) aur Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne ek shakhs ko Khaibar par aamil muqarrar kiya, wo umdah khajoor lekar aaya. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne poocha: Kya sab khajoorein Khaibar ki aisi hi hoti hain? Usne kaha: Nahi Ya Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam), hum is khajoor mein se ek saa do saa ke badle mein ya do saa teen saa ke badle mein khareed liya karte hain. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Aisa na karo, pehle buri khajoor ko rupayon ke badle mein bech kar phir umdah khajoor rupay dekar khareed lo.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1311]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1311" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1298

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1312)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ يَزِيدَ، أَنَّ زَيْدًا أَبَا عَيَّاشٍ، أَخْبَرَهُ أَنَّهُ، سَأَلَ سَعْدَ بْنَ أَبِي وَقَّاصٍ عَنِ الْبَيْضَاءِ، بِالسُّلْتِ فَقَالَ لَهُ سَعْدٌ أَيَّتُهُمَا أَفْضَلُ قَالَ الْبَيْضَاءُ ‏.‏ فَنَهَاهُ عَنْ ذَلِكَ ‏.‏ وَقَالَ سَعْدٌ سَمِعْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يُسْأَلُ عَنِ اشْتِرَاءِ التَّمْرِ بِالرُّطَبِ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ أَيَنْقُصُ الرُّطَبُ إِذَا يَبِسَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَقَالُوا نَعَمْ ‏.‏ فَنَهَى عَنْ ذَلِكَ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1312]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1312" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abdullah ibn Yazid that Zayd ibn Ayyash told him that he had once asked Sad ibn Abi Waqqas about selling white wheat for a type of good barley. Sad asked him which was the better and when he told him the white wheat, he forbade the transaction. Sad said, &quot;I heard the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, being asked about selling dried dates for fresh dates, and the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;Do the dates diminish in size when they become dry?&#x27; When he was told that they did, he forbade that</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1312]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1312" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1312 ]</p>

</details>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">ज़ैद बिन अबू अय्यश से रिवायत है के उन्होंने साद बिन आबि वक़्क़ास (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से पुछा के जो हिजाज़ में पैदा होता है उसके बदले में बेच सकते हैं? उन्होंने कहा: दोनों में कौनसा अच्छा है? वो बोले: जो. तोह मन किया इस से और साद ने कहा मैंने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से सुना पुछा के खुश्क खजूर को रातब (ताज़ा) के बदले में बेचना कैसा है? आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: रातब जब सूख जाता है तोह वज़न कम हो जाता है? लोगों ने कहा: हाँ. तोह आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने मन फ़रमाया.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1312]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1312" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">زید بن ابوعیاش سے روایت ہے کہ انہوں نے پوچھا سعد بن ابی وقاص سے کہ جو کے غور اور حجاز میں پیدا ہوتا ہے کے بدلے میں بیچ سکتے ہیں انہوں نے کہا دونوں میں کونسا اچھا ہے بولے جو تو منع کیا اس سے اور سعد نے کہا میں نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے سنا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے لوگوں سے پوچھا کہ خشک کھجور کو رطب بدلے میں بیچنا کیسا ہے آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا رطب جب سوکھ جاتا ہے تو وزن اس کا کم ہو جاتا ہے لوگوں نے کہا ہاں آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے منع فرمایا ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1312]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1312" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Zaid bin Abu Ayyash se riwayat hai ke unhone Saad bin Abi Waqqas (Radi Allahu Anhu) se poocha ke jo Hijaz mein paida hota hai uske badle mein bech sakte hain? Unhone kaha: Dono mein kaunsa achha hai? Wo bole: Jo. Toh mana kiya is se aur Saad ne kaha maine Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se suna, aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne logon se poocha ke khushk khajoor ko ratab (taaza) ke badle mein bechna kaisa hai? Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Ratab jab sookh jata hai toh wazan kam ho jata hai? Logon ne kaha: Haan. Toh aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne mana farmaya.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1312]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1312" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1299

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1313)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم نَهَى عَنِ الْمُزَابَنَةِ وَالْمُزَابَنَةُ بَيْعُ الثَّمَرِ بِالتَّمْرِ كَيْلاً وَبَيْعُ الْكَرْمِ بِالزَّبِيبِ كَيْلاً ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1313]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1313" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi from Abdullah ibn Umar that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, forbade muzabana. Muzabana was selling fresh dates for dried dates by measure, and selling grapes for raisins by measure</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1313]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1313" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने मज़बाना से मन फ़रमाया. मज़बाना उसको कहते हैं के दरख़्त पर फल (खजूर या अंगूर) का अंदाज़ा करके खुश्क खजूर या अंगूर के बदले में फरोख्त की जाये.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1313]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے منع کیا مزابنہ اس کو کہتے ہیں کہ درخت پر پھل کھجور یا انگور اندزہ کر کے خشک کھجور یا انگور کے بدلے میں فروخت کی جائیں ۔</p>

## Narration 1300

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1314)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ دَاوُدَ بْنِ الْحُصَيْنِ، عَنْ أَبِي سُفْيَانَ، مَوْلَى ابْنِ أَبِي أَحْمَدَ عَنْ أَبِي سَعِيدٍ الْخُدْرِيِّ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم نَهَى عَنِ الْمُزَابَنَةِ وَالْمُحَاقَلَةِ وَالْمُزَابَنَةُ اشْتِرَاءُ الثَّمَرِ بِالتَّمْرِ فِي رُءُوسِ النَّخْلِ وَالْمُحَاقَلَةُ كِرَاءُ الأَرْضِ بِالْحِنْطَةِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1314]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1314" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Da&#x27;ud ibn al-Husayn from Abu Sufyan, the mawla of Ibn Abi Ahmad, from Abu Said al-Khudri that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, forbade muzabana and muhaqala. Muzabana was selling fresh dates for dried dates while they were still on the trees. Muhaqala was renting land in exchange for wheat</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1314]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1314" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू सईद खुदरी (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने मज़बाना और मुहकला से मना फ़रमाया. मज़बाना के मानि ऊपर बयान हुए और मुहकला उसको कहते हैं कि गेहूं का खेत बदले में खुश्क गेहूं के बचे.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1314]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1314" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابو سعید خدری سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے منع کیا مزابنہ اور محاقلہ سے مزابنہ کے معنی اوپر بیان ہوئے اور محاقلہ اس کو کہتے ہیں کہ گہیوں کا کھیت بدلے میں خشک گہیوں کے بیچے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1314]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1314" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>
